([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/stratified-fold-partitioning-of-reference-sequences))
- **stratified-fraction-aggregation-by-cell-type-and-length**: Use when a per-call result table already exists (e.g., classified Ginkgo CNA calls in 65 PTA-amplified brain cells) and the analysis goal is summary fractions broken down by cell type and call-length bins, as in HiScanner Fig. 4d–j. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/stratified-fraction-aggregation-by-cell-type-and-length))
- **stratified-holdout-cross-validation**: Use when benchmarking a supervised cell-type classifier (e.g., SciBet vs scmap vs Seurat v3) on labeled scRNA-seq datasets and a repeated, class-balanced hold-out estimate of classification accuracy is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/stratified-holdout-cross-validation))

([Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy](https://github.com/Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/stratified-k-fold) · ★1)
- **stratified-molecule-subampling-per-chromatographic-method**: Use when validating few-shot RT projection to a chromatographic method with limited identified molecules — e.g. testing meta-learned GP projections at 10, 20, 25, 30, 40, 50 training points. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/stratified-molecule-subampling-per-chromatographic-method))
- **stratified-quota-sampling**: Stratify a long-tailed dataset by normalizing raw counts via Box-Cox (or Yeo-Johnson for zero-containing data), bin into tiers at standard deviation cutoffs, then allocate quotas per tier. Never log-then-Box-Cox (redundant — Box-Cox contains log at lambda=0). Check dimension correlation via Cramer's V before multi-axis stratification. Allocate over cross-product cells, not per-dimension. ([thistleknot/skills](https://github.com/thistleknot/skills/tree/HEAD/stratified-quota-sampling))
- **stratified-random-test-set-sampling**: Decision support for comparing CCS prediction algorithms on a common footing: when benchmarking CCSP 2.0, CCSbase, AllCCS or DeepCCS against the same 463 McLean CCS Compendium measurements, or designing stratified hold-out splits for IM-MS regression evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/stratified-random-test-set-sampling))
- **stratified-summary-statistics**: Use when you have paired CCS (or other instrument-reported quantity) measurements from two methods/software for the same compounds and need per-stratum (mode, acquisition setting) average absolute relative bias summaries and a tabulated output. - **stratified-train-test-split-metabolomics**: Use when building predictive models (e.g. RF or elastic-net classifiers) from a metabolite-by-sample intensity table with class labels, and you need class-proportioned train/test separation before feature selection and AUC evaluation.
